みずほ経済 EXPRESS

みずほ GDP ナウ（2026 年 10 月中旬時点） 
～7～9 月期 GDP は前期比＋0.03％（年率＋0.10％）と推計～

調査部  シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山祐資 
同             エコノミスト  阿部大樹 
080-1069-4835 yusuke.koshiyama@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 10 月中旬時点の「みずほ GDP ナウ」は 7～9 月期 GDP を前期比＋0.10％と予測 
みずほ総合研究所では景気動向をタイムリーに把握するべく、浦沢（2023）等を参考にGDPナウキ
ャスティングに取り組んできた。これを「みずほGDPナウ」と呼称することとし、太田他（2024）で
はデータドリブン予測である「みずほGDPナウ」が民間予測平均並みの予測精度を確保できることを
示した。その上で、酒井他（2024）をはじめとして、月次経済指標を用いたGDPナウキャスティング
の結果を紹介してきたところである。 
本稿では10月中旬時点までに得られる月次経済指標を用い、 「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期GDP
の予測結果を紹介する。予測に用いたデータは、8月分の鉱工業生産、 消費活動指数、 所定外労働時間、
消費財出荷指数、第3次産業活動指数、9月分の中小企業景況調査（売上げ見通しDI）である1。図表1
には、予測に使用した各月次経済指標の推移を示している。 
図表 1 各月次経済指標の推移

（注）青字は改善、赤字は悪化を示す

### Page 2

2

モデルによる10月中旬時点における7 ～9月期実質GDP成長率の予測値は、図表2のとおり前期比＋
0.03％ （年率＋0.10％）となった。 他機関による予測では、東京財団政策研究所による10月15日時点の
ナウキャスティングが同＋0.29％（年率＋1.18％）、日本経済研究センターが公表した10月のESPフォ
ーキャスト調査（回答期間：2026年9月30日～10月7日、回答者37名）における民間予測値平均が前期
比年率＋0.08％となっている。「みずほGDPナウ」の予測値は、民間コンセンサスよりやや高いもの
の、概ね近い伸びを予測していると言える。 
なお、多くの民間エコノミストが保守的な見方をしている理由の一つには、GDP統計作成上のテク
ニカルな要因がある。4～6月期には、 中東情勢悪化を背景に原油 ・ ナフサ輸入が大幅に減少したほか、
石油化学製品の供給途絶懸念から幅広い企業で在庫積み増しの動きが生じた。やや直感に反するが、
いずれもGDPに対しては押し上げ要因となる。 そのため、4～6月期GDPは前期比＋0.36％ （年率＋1.45％）
とプラス成長になったものの、 成長率が示すほど景気の実態は強くなかったとみるのが自然であろう。
反対に、7～9月期については、4～6月期の反動で輸入増・在庫減の動きが顕在化し、GDPには一定の
下方圧力がかかる可能性がある。みずほ総合研究所が9月に公表したレポート2でも、こうしたテクニ
カル要因を考慮の上、7～9月期GDPは横ばい圏の伸びにとどまると予想している。 
■ 「みずほ GDP ナウ」による予測値下方修正の主因は生産・消費関連指標の悪化 
「みずほGDPナウ」 による7～9月期のGDP（1次速報値） の予測にあたっては10月中旬までに公表さ
れた月次経済指標を使って予測値を4回更新した。図表3では、予測値の更新過程と各月次経済指標の
寄与度を示している3。予測値が下方修正された主因は、8月の鉱工業生産、 消費財出荷指数の低下だ。
特に鉱工業生産指数については、7月末に発生した熊本地震による影響が下押し要因になったとみら
れる。 大手自動車メーカーで工場の操業が停止した影響で自動車が前月比▲6.8％と大幅に減産したほ
か、 電子部品 ・ デバイスも同▲1.9％の減産となった。7月調査時点の生産予測指数は前月比＋3.1％（ 補
図表 2 各機関の実質GDP成長率予測値 図表 3 予測値の改定過程と各経済指標の寄与度

ト調査」等より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

前に予測しており （モデル予測値） 、 寄与度はモデル予測値と

### Page 3

3

正値ベース）と増産が見込まれていたが、大幅に下振れた格好だ。一方、世界的に旺盛なAI・データ
センター関連需要を背景に、半導体製造装置を含む生産用機械などが増産したものの、熊本地震の影
響を打ち返せなかった。消費財出荷指数の低下については、4～5月の耐久財特需が一巡したことで反
動減の動きが生じた。非耐久財の出荷は前月から増加したものの、7月の落ち込みも踏まえると、 基調
としては力強さを欠いていると言えよう。長雨や低温、といった天候不順により生鮮野菜価格が上昇
しているほか、食肉も値上がりが大きく、こうした手に取る機会が多い品目の価格上昇が家計の節約
志向を強め、個人消費の下押し圧力となっているとみられる。9月についても、食品のほか、原油・石
油化学製品等のコスト高の影響で幅広い品目の価格上昇が加速する見込みであり、個人消費は冴えな
い動きが続く可能性がある。 
以上のように、7～9月期成長率は低い伸びに抑えられる公算が大きい。前述のとおり原油・ナフサ
の輸入増、 企業の在庫調整といった動きはGDP統計上、成長率に対してマイナスに寄与する。加えて、
熊本地震による生産減や天候要因による消費支出抑制の動きが下押し要因になる見込みだ。 
次回の 「みずほGDPナウ」については、11月16日に公表される7～9月期GDP1次速報を挟んで、10～
12月期GDPのリアルタイム予測に取り組む。10月分の鉱工業生産や消費活動指数の結果等を踏まえて
推計し、12月中旬頃のレポート発刊を予定している。原油高等を受けた物価上昇圧力の強まりや堅調
なAI・データセンター関連需要など、プラス・マイナス双方の影響が交錯する中、ナウキャストによ
る景気動向の把握はより重要なものとなるだろう。

1 ステップワイズ法により使用データを採択した。詳細は太田ほか（2024）を参照されたい。今後のモデルの予測精度のパフォ
ーマンス評価等を踏まえ、採択するデータについては見直しを行う可能性がある。 
2 詳細については、服部直樹「年率＋1.4％と 1 次速報から小幅に上方修正（4～6 月期2 次QE）」（2026 年9 月8 日）を参照され
たい。 
3 太田他（2024）で示した枠組みと同様、図表 2 の折れ線が各時点における実質GDP 成長率の予測値であり、月次の経済指標が
新たに公表されたり改定されたりすることで予測値が更新される。棒グラフは、予測値の改定幅、すなわち前回予測との差を各
月次経済指標で寄与度分解したもので、寄与度を合計するとモデル予測値の改定幅と一致する。なお、 「みずほGDP ナウ」は各
月次経済指標についても公表前に予測値を計算しており、各指標の寄与度はモデル予測値と実績値の乖離から計算されることか
ら、寄与度の符号は経済指標の変化の方向とは必ずしも一致しない。

金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 9 月 30 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國企業投資動能維持韌性 ：8 月標普全球美國綜合 PMI 升至 58.4，創2021 年 7 月來高點 ，
製造業、服務業同步擴張至 57 和 58.7； 8 月扣除飛機與國防的核心資本財訂單月增 1.6%，
高於前月修正後的 0.6%。其中，初級金屬、機械及電機設備訂單均呈現成長，顯示企業資本
支出、AI 投資與國防需求仍具韌性，持續支撐美國經濟成長。
◼ 美中同意貿易休兵再延長兩個月：美中同意調降雙邊總值 600 億美元商品的關稅，涵蓋美國

玉米、化妝品及中國家電、玩具等產品，並延長貿易休兵至明年 1 月 10 日，同時中國承諾繼
續供應稀土並擴大採購美國農產品與煤礦，美國則繼續暫停實施 50%穿透性規則；AI 方面，
美中雙方雖未就 AI 監管議題達成共識 ， 但同意建立風險溝通機制 ， 並於11 月底前展開對話 。
◼ Meta 旗下 AI 代理人 Muse 登頂排行榜：Meta 新款個人 AI 代理 Muse 上線後迅速累積逾
250 萬次下載，並登上美國 App Store 下載排行榜首位。相較過去生成式 AI 以問答與內容生
成為主，Muse 可進一步協助安排日程、搜尋資訊、處理文件及完成交易，代表 AI 正從提升
效率工具逐步走向數位勞動力，市場評價焦點也將逐漸由 AI 基礎設施擴散至應用軟體業者。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，科技供應鏈成長動能延續：AI 基礎建設需求持續擴張，帶動 HBM、伺服器
DRAM、 先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持成長動能 ， 市場表現由全面上漲逐步轉向結構性分
化。若 AI 伺服器需求持續成長且 CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優勢、定價能力及較高訂單

能見度之企業，其營運表現有機會獲得支撐。整體而言，科技產業獲利表現仍具關注度，惟後
續仍須留意全球景氣、產業供需變化、政策監管及市場評價調整等風險因素。
◼ 債券佈局可關注中天期美國公債及投等債：中天期美國公債及投資級債仍值得持續關注：**雖
然貨幣政策風險下降有助於穩定長期通膨預期 ， 但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應 ，
以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，
中天期債券於票息收益與存續期間風險之間相對較為均衡。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公
司債指數利差約 80 個基點，雖然利差偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供相對較高的收益水準。
因此，在目前市場環境下，中天期美國公債及投資級債仍為值得持續觀察的債券資產類別，惟
後續表現仍須留意利率走勢、信用利差變化及經濟成長動能等因素。

### Page 2

本報告係根據研究資料及各項可靠數據作成，兆豐銀行保留報告內容所有權利，未經本行許可，請勿引

1

國際經濟金融週報內容摘要 
美 
國 
美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
經濟 
⚫ 2026 年第 2 季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值上修至 2.2%， 惟低於第1 季
之 2.5%。成長主要由個人消費及固定投資帶動，其中消費貢獻 2.51 個
百分點；惟進口大增 12.6%，致淨出口拖累 1.10 個百分點。另實質私人
國內最終銷售成長 4.6%，顯示內需仍具韌性。 
⚫ 9 月美國非農就業僅增 2.9 萬人，遠低於前月及近一年平均，失業率升
至 4.2%， 薪資增幅續降 ， 顯示企業聘僱動能轉弱 。 惟勞動市場尚未明顯
惡化，後續仍須關注就業降溫對聯準會利率決策之影響。 
⚫ 8 月美國職位空缺數由 733.5 萬個降至 707.9 萬個，職缺率降至 4.3%，
顯示企業徵才需求略為降溫；惟聘僱人數增至 519.2 萬人。主動離職及
裁員規模同步下降，反映勞工轉職意願與企業人力調整趨於審慎，勞動
市場供需大致維持平衡。 
⚫ 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數為 54.5，連續第九個月維持擴張。
新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示需求仍具韌性；惟生產、出
口及進口動能略為放緩，且原物料成本持續攀升，加上關稅政策、地緣
政治及供應鏈不確定性仍存，製造業仍面臨部分壓力。 
⚫ 8 月美國 PCE 物價指數月增 0.3%、年增 3.4%，核心 PCE 月增 0.2%、
年增 3.0%， 顯示通膨大致持穩但短期物價壓力略升 。 另一方面 ， 個人消
費支出月增 0.9%、實質消費成長 0.6%，帶動儲蓄率降至 4.1%，顯示家
庭需求仍具韌性；後續須關注消費熱度及通膨對聯準會政策之影響。 
重要 
事件 
⚫ 近期聯準會官員立場趨於分歧，理事 Michael Barr 認為能源價格及 AI
投資熱潮可能延緩通膨降溫 ， 未來仍可能進一步升息 ； 惟John Williams、
Philip Jefferson 及 Michelle Bowman 等官員則主張暫無急於升息的必要 ，
傾向觀察後續經濟數據。加以 9 月非農就業明顯放緩，市場對 10 月升
息預期大幅降溫，焦點轉向 12 月會議。 
⚫ 台積電傳評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作赴德州設廠，雙方仍處討論階
段。合作可能由台積電主導營運，或由 Terafab 持多數股權、台積電提
供技術 。 若成真 ， 將擴大台積電美國布局 ， 並支援Tesla、SpaceX 及 xAI
晶片需求。

一、 美國—美國經濟維持溫和擴張，勞動市場降溫與通膨黏著牽動聯準會政策 
2026 年第二季美國實質 GDP 季增年率終值由前次估計之 1.5%上修至 2.2%，
惟仍低於第一季之 2.5%， 顯示經濟持續擴張但成長動能略有放緩 。 此次上修主要
反映投資、消費及政府支出優於原先估計。就成長結構觀察，個人消費支出仍為
主要動能，貢獻 2.51 個百分點，其中服務及商品消費分別貢獻 1.57 及 0.94 個百
分點；私人投資貢獻 0.82 個百分點，以設備投資及智慧財產權投資表現強勁，惟
庫存調整拖累 0.53 個百分點。對外貿易方面，出口成長 5.0%，但進口大幅增加
12.6%， 致淨出口拖累GDP 成長 1.10 個百分點 。 另反映內需基本面的實質私人國
內最終銷售成長 4.6%， 高於第一季之1.8%， 顯示民間需求明顯增強 。 物價方面 ，
第 2 季 PCE 及核心 PCE 物價指數分別上升 5.0%及 3.3%， 顯示通膨壓力仍偏高 。
整體而言，美國經濟仍由消費與固定投資支撐，惟進口快速增加及通膨黏著性，
仍為後續經濟前景的重要觀察重點。 
2026 年 9 月美國非農就業人數增加 2.9 萬人 ， 明顯低於上月修正後的13.3 萬
人，也低於過去 12 個月平均每月增加 4.5 萬人的水準；失業率小幅升至 4.2%，
整體仍維持在 3 月以來 4.1%至 4.3%的區間，反映勞動市場整體變化有限，惟新
增就業力道已轉趨疲弱。家庭調查方面，勞動參與率升至 61.8%，就業人口比為
59.2%，均較上月小幅上升；長期失業人數約 194.4 萬人，占總失業人口 27.1%，
大致維持穩定。產業別方面，主要產業就業變化有限，其中醫療保健增加 1.7 萬
人、營建業增加 1.1 萬人、製造業增加 0.9 萬人，金融活動則減少 0.7 萬人，政府
部門減少 1.7 萬人。薪資方面，私人部門平均時薪升至 37.81 美元，月增 0.1%、
年增 3.0%，平均每週工時維持 34.4 小時。另 7 月與 8 月新增就業合計下修 6 萬
人，反映前二月就業動能較初估疲弱。整體而言，9 月美國新增就業明顯放緩、
失業率小幅上升且薪資增速降溫，企業聘僱動能較先前轉弱；惟失業率仍維持於
近期區間，勞動市場尚未出現明顯惡化跡象，後續仍須關注企業聘僱需求是否持
續降溫，以及對聯準會利率決策的影響。 
2026 年 8 月美國職位空缺數(Job Openings) 由上月修正後之 733.5 萬個減少
25.6 萬個至 707.9 萬個 ， 職缺率由4.4%降至 4.3%， 顯示企業用人需求略有降溫 ；

惟聘僱人數由 514.6 萬人小幅增加至 519.2 萬人，聘僱率由 3.2%升至 3.3%。總離
職人數由 512.8 萬人減少至 507.0 萬人，離職率維持於 3.2%。就離職結構觀察，
主動離職(Quits)人數由 308.9 萬人降至 306.6 萬人，主動離職率維持 1.9%；裁員
與解僱(Layoffs and Discharges) 人數則由 170.2 萬人降至 164.1 萬人，裁員率由
1.1%降至 1.0%。產業別方面，職位空缺增加主要來自零售業、金融保險業、資訊
業及休閒餐飲業 ； 聘僱人數則以政府部門及製造業增幅較為明顯 。 整體而言 ，8 月
美國職缺數較前月下滑，反映企業徵才需求略有降溫，但聘僱活動仍維持穩定，
且主動離職與裁員規模同步下降，顯示勞工轉職意願及企業人力調整趨於審慎，
勞動市場供需仍大致維持平衡。 
2026 年 9 月美國 ISM 製造業採購經理人指數(Manufacturing PMI) 為 54.5，
較上月之 54.6 微降 0.1 點，惟已連續第九個月維持於 50 以上擴張區間，顯示製
造業景氣持續擴張。細項觀察，新訂單指數由53.7 升至 55.3，需求動能續增；生
產指數由 58.3 降至 56.7，雖仍維持擴張，但成長速度略有放緩；就業指數由51.2
升至 52.7，反映企業聘僱需求持續增加。供應商交貨指數由 59.3 微降至 59.0，交
貨延遲情況仍存；庫存指數則由 50.6 降至 48.6，企業備貨態度轉趨保守。此外，
價格指數由 71.1 升至 77.9，原物料及投入成本壓力升高；未完成訂單指數由51.8
升至 56.4，訂單去化變慢。另一方面，新出口訂單指數由53.2 降至 50.9，進口指
數亦由 52.5 降至 51.0，外需成長動能及企業備料需求略為放緩。整體而言，9 月
美國製造業活動持續穩健擴張，新訂單、就業及未完成訂單同步改善，顯示製造
業景氣仍具韌性；惟出口及進口增速放緩、投入成本壓力升高，加上關稅政策、
地緣政治風險及供應鏈不確定性仍存，後續發展仍值得持續關注。 
2026 年 8 月美國個人消費支出(PCE)物價指數月增率由上月之 0.1%升至 0.3%，
年增率維持於 3.4%； 扣除食品及能源後之核心PCE 月增率由 0.1%升至 0.2%，年
增率亦持平於 3.0%，顯示整體通膨大致持穩，惟短期物價漲勢較前月略有回升。
另一方面，個人所得月增 0.2%，可支配所得(DPI)月增 0.3%，實質可支配所得持
平；個人消費支出月增 0.9%，實質個人消費支出成長 0.6%，顯示民間消費表現
明顯回溫。由於消費增幅高於所得增幅，個人儲蓄率由前月之 4.6%降至 4.1%。
整體而言，8 月美國通膨表現大致持穩，但消費支出顯著回升，反映家庭需求仍
具韌性；後續仍須持續關注消費熱度、通膨走勢及其對聯準會貨幣政策路徑之影
響。

近期聯準會官員談話呈現鷹鴿分歧。聯準會理事 Michael Barr 認為，中東局
勢推升能源價格，以及 AI 相關投資熱潮帶動需求增長，均可能阻礙通膨回落至
2%目標 ， 未來仍不排除進一步升息的必要性 ； 惟 紐約聯準銀行總裁 John Williams、
聯準會副主席 Philip Jefferson 及金融監理副主席 Michelle Bowman 則強調，在 9
月升息後，當前並無急迫性立即進一步收緊政策，應先觀察後續通膨、就業及金
融市場數據變化，再評估是否有進一步調整利率的必要。受官員談話轉趨審慎，
以及 9 月非農就業表現疲弱影響，市場對 10 月 FOMC 會議再度升息的預期明顯
降溫，政策焦點逐漸轉向 12 月會議及年底前是否仍有一次升息空間。 
晶圓代工龍頭台積電傳出正評估與馬斯克旗下 Terafab 合作，在美國德州布
局晶圓廠，馬斯克也於社群平台 X 證實雙方正在討論，並表示 「目前僅在討論階
段 ， 但可能會有進展」 。 目前浮上檯面的合作模式包括由台積電持有並營運新廠 ，
SpaceX 或 Terafab 透過入股及承諾採購晶圓參與；另一方案則由 Terafab 掌握多
數股權 ， 台積電提供製程技術與晶圓廠營運經驗 。Terafab 旨在因應 Tesla、SpaceX
及 xAI 快速成長的 AI 晶片需求，首期投資規模達 168 億美元。若合作成真，不
僅可望成為台積電繼亞利桑那州後在美國的新布局，也凸顯先進製程與穩定晶片
供應的重要性，但目前具體合作方式、時程與投資內容仍未正式定案。 
二、歐元區—法國殖利率大幅上升，不利於投資及消費表現 
 歐元區 9 月 HICP 通膨 、 核心通膨(不含食品及能源)分別上升 0.6、0.1 個百分
點至 3.8%、2.5%， 主因國際原油價格上漲 ， 使能源通膨上升4.5 個百分點至 18.8%，
並透過肥料、物流成本上漲，間接推升食品通膨 0.3 個百分點至 1.4%。另外，服
務通膨上升 0.2 個百分點至 3.2%，近三個月服務通膨大致呈現橫向波動，部分反
映旅遊旺季因素影響。基於布蘭特原油持續位於每桶 90 美元以上，預期年底前
仍將對通膨帶來額外壓力，後續須關注能源漲價可能會透過運輸、物流等成本管
道傳導至核心商品及服務價格，進而提高核心通膨黏性。 
 其他經濟數據包含 ：(1)歐元區 8 月失業率持平 6.4%， 失業人數增加2.6 萬人 ，
主要是義大利勞動參與人數增加所致，不過 ，基於PMI 顯示近期德國勞動雇用需
求持續增溫，將有助於穩定勞動市場表現；(2)歐元區第二季家庭儲蓄率下降 0.2
個百分點至 14.2%，顯示通膨壓力削弱民眾購買力，致儲蓄暫時性減少；(3)歐元
區第二季房價年增率為 4%， 較第一季降0.6 個百分點 ， 主因法國中古屋面臨嚴格

環保法規，須負擔高昂翻修成本，使法國房價呈現連三季下滑所拖累。 
 近一週法國 10 年期公債殖利率升至 4.9%以上，與德國公債殖利率差距擴大
至 125 個基點以上，主要反映市場對法國主權公債可持續性疑慮升溫。儘管目前
政府計畫將 2027 年財政赤字縮限至 5%，惟政府在國會並無穩定多數席次，即使
能以行政命令強行通過預算 ， 也可能面臨倒閣風險 ；此外 ，市場對於2027 年 4 月
總統大選後，新總統能否取得國會足夠支持，推動削減赤字與控制債務的改革亦
缺乏信心。因此未來法國仍可能面臨殖利率進一步上升與財政壓力加劇的風險，
暫時不利於投資及消費信心。 
三、日本—景氣溫和復甦，但消費放緩與通膨升溫風險升高 
 8 月工業生產月減 1.7%，主要係受到該月地震及豪雨等天然災害影響，導致
部分工廠暫時停產。從細項來看，汽車業受停工影響最為明顯，產量下滑6.8%，
電子產品產量亦減少 1.9%。 惟生產機械產量連續三個月成長 ， 加上核心資本財出
貨持續增加，顯示企業投資需求仍相當強勁，有助於支撐後續工業生產回升。從
企業預期來看，製造商預期9 月產量將成長 3.2%，顯示生產活動可望迅速回溫，
在供應鏈逐步恢復、庫存回補需求持續，以及穩健的企業資本支出支撐下，預計
第三季工業生產仍可維持正成長，且成長動能可望延續至年底。 
 8 月零售月減 1.2%，雖然表現略優於市場預期，但仍顯示民間消費動能正在
放緩。此次疲弱主要原因來自耐久財銷售下滑，其中家電銷售因空調等產品在換
機需求告一段落後明顯回落，汽車銷售也隨政府刺激措施效應逐漸消退而連續第
四個月下降。相較之下，食品、服裝等非耐久財銷售雖同步走弱，但降幅相對有
限 ， 反映薪資成長仍對消費提供部分支撐 。惟不利天候與通膨壓力仍是主要風險 ，
8 月至 9 月持續豪雨已影響服裝等非必需消費，而除米價以外的食品及商品價格
持續上漲，也正侵蝕家庭實質購買力，恐限制消費者信心與消費復甦力道。 
 9 月東京核心通膨年增率升至 2.7%，雖然東京都免費托兒及免費水費政策造
成的基期效應貢獻約 0.5 個百分點，但通膨升溫主因仍是價格上漲已由能源與食
品擴散至整體經濟。受石油價格上揚帶動，企業開始加速將原物料及薪資成本轉
嫁給消費者，帶動商品與服務價格普遍走高；租金通膨更升至 2%，創 1994 年以
來新高，公共交通及醫療等項目也開始調漲。隨著東京的物價壓力逐步反映至全
國數據，日本央行進一步升息的可能性升高。

四、中國—為刺激內需、引導產業走向，人行漸近式放寬政策工具 
9 月官方製造業 PMI 上升 0.3 點至 50.1，為近三個月高點，新訂單、出口訂
單指數分別為 50.5、50，並帶動生產指數上升至 51.7，不過，庫存指數下降、就
業指數加速衰退、企業展望減弱，皆拖累 PMI 指數水準。此外，投入、產出物價
指數分別上升至 60.8、54，顯示成本壓力加劇，但售價調整速度依然較為遲緩，
不利於獲利表現。官方非製造業 PMI 上升 1.2 點至 50.2，其中建築業活動指數大
增 3.4 點至 50.3，脫離連續八個月的衰退區間，主要是政府加大財政刺激力度，
推動重大建設開工；非製造業的從業人員指數為 45.9，顯示就業情況仍顯嚴峻，
亦較不利於內需表現。 
鑒於內需動能疲弱，人行於 9 月 29 日宣布下調抵押補充貸款(該貸款本質是
將資金引導至政府希望發展的領域)利率自 1.75%降至 1.50%，並擴大適用範圍，
將水網、新型電網、算力網、新一代通信網、城市地下管網及物流網等建設納入
支持，有助於提振內需動能。未來若內需復甦力道不如預期，不排除人行將進一
步降準或擴大結構性貨幣工具支持經濟；至於全面降息則仍須兼顧人民幣匯率及
中美利差等因素，可能性相對較低。 
五、印度及東南亞—泰國、菲律賓出口均達 2 成以上的高速成長 
(1)新加坡：受服務業、零售暨其他商品業，以及食品價格漲幅擴大影響，8 月消
費者物價指數(CPI)年增率升至 2.3%、不包括住宿及個人交通費用之核心 CPI 年
增率升至 2.2%， 連續3 個月上升並創 2 年新高 。 受全球能源價格居高不下及氣候
條件不佳影響，預期新加坡進口食品、商品及服務成本將持續上升，惟國內生產
率成長及政府補貼政策將抑制部分服務業通膨，新加坡金管局(MAS)或在 10 月
「略微」緊縮貨幣政策。(2)泰國：8 月出口額年增 24.3%(346 億美元)，主要由工

件（151.2%） 及電路板 （19.2%） 增幅最為顯著，對美國出口年增48.7%、對中國
和日本分別成長 15.5%、19.6%。8 月進口額年增 25.1%(371 億美元)，以燃料 （年
增 42.8%） 、 資本貨物 （33.9%） 、 其他貨物 （27.4%） 、 原材料和中間產品 （21.3%）、
運輸設備（13.6%）為主。進出口相抵，8 月貿易逆差 24.8 億美元，較 7 月略收
窄，為連續第 11 個月出現逆差。(3)菲律賓：8 月出口額大增 27.8%(91 億美元)，
創兩年新高，主要得益於電子產品（年增 59.9%） ，尤其是半導體（73.5%）及黃

稅後 EPS 為 9.04 元 。 隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、 Vera Rubin 產品 量產出貨 ， 預期 4Q26 營運
將有顯著提升 。 受 惠 於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ，

3Q26 稅後 EPS 為 9.0 4 元 ：  
台 達 電 公 布 3Q26 營 收 為 1, 970.59 億 元 、 QoQ+ 7.5%； 因 提 列 庫 存 跌 價 損 失
影響 ， 毛 利 率 降 至 34.1% ， 營 業 利 益 率 為 16.6 %； 稅 後 淨 利 為 234.9 5 億 元 、
QoQ -6.5 %， 稅 後 EPS 為 9. 04 元 ， 低 於 預 期 。  
產 品 規 格 提 升 ， 電源 類 產 品 展 望 佳：  
台達電 為 AI 伺 服 器 電 源 解 決 方 案 主 要 供 應 商， 受 惠 於 AI 需 求 提 升 ， 帶 動
AC/DC 電 源 大 幅 成 長 外 ，CSP 客 戶 在 新 一 代 ASIC 伺 服 器 平 台 採 用 DC/DC
電 源 模 組 ， 亦 成 為 另 一 成 長 動 能 。800 VDC 與 ± 400VDC 預 計 於 4Q26 出貨 ，
2026 年 營 收 比 重 約 1% ，預 期 2H27 營 收 貢 獻 較 顯 著。  
水 冷 散 熱 2026 年 營 收 維 持 成 長 趨 勢：  
水冷散熱產品有水冷板、 Manifold 、 CDU 、 Sidecar 。目前出貨以系統類的
Sidecar 為 主 ， 歸 類 於 基 礎 建 設； CDU 則 歸 類 於 電 源 類 與 零 組 件 。 水 冷 散 熱
2025 年 營 收 比 重 為 9%，台 達 電 Sidecar 產品 2025 年 市 佔 率 極 高，雖 有 競 爭
者 加 入 ， 但 整 體 市 場 需 求 持 續 增 長 ，2026 年 Sidecar 營 收 仍 將 維 持 成 長 趨
勢，預估 2026 年 水 冷 散 熱 營 收 比 重 為 12% 。  
固 態 變 壓 器 為 長 期 成 長 動 能：  
SST (固 態 變 壓器 )目 前 已 少 量 出 貨，客 戶 為 中 系 CSP 、800V 高 壓 直 流 電 動 車
充 電 站，亦 出 貨 給 美 系 客 戶 測 試。SST 導 入 需 從 資 料 中 心 設 計 初 期 即 納 入 規
劃，預期  2~3 年 後 會 產 生 顯 著 的 營 收 貢 獻 ， 仍 持 續 改 良 產 品 規 格 中 。 
投 資 建 議 ：  
隨 缺 料 情 況 改 善、Vera Rubin 產 品 量 產 出 貨，預 期 4Q26 營 運 將 有 顯 著 提 升。
受惠於 AI 、 Data Center 領 域 需 求 成 長 ， 營 收 與 獲 利 維 持 成 長 趨 勢 ， 投 資 評
等為 Buy 。

2 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

### Page 1

Summary: Ardentec released its 3Q26 results and held its investor meeting after market 
hours on 27 October. EPS of TWD3.1 beat our estimate (TWD3.04) and the consensus 
(TWD2.89) by 2.1% and 7.3%, respectively. Operating profit was broadly in line with our 
forecast, but beat consensus by 5%, driven by the Longtan plant ramp-up and higher 
utilisation rates.

We have a Buy (1) rating and 12M TP of TWD395, based on target PER of 25x on our 2027E 
EPS.

What’s the impact:

• Stronger-than-expected 3Q26 gross margin: Ardentec’s 3Q26 revenue reached 
TWD5,337m (+17.7% QoQ, +48.5% YoY), broadly in line with both our estimate and the 
consensus. The strong revenue growth was primarily driven by volume ramp-up at the 
Longtan facilities and supported by spillover demand. Gross margin came in at 45.4% 
(+5.2ppt QoQ, +9.7ppt YoY), beating our forecast and consensus expectations by 0.3ppt 
and 1.7ppt, respectively, mainly due to the Longtan plant ramp-up and higher utilisation 
rates (approximately 80%). Operating income was TWD1,860m (+35.0% QoQ, +112.8% 
YoY), largely in line with our estimate, but beating the consensus by 5.1%. Net profit of 
TWD1,473m, largely in line with both our estimate and the consensus. Although 3Q26 
EPS came in at TWD3.1, beating our estimate and the consensus by 2.1% and 7.3%, 
respectively, the variance was mainly due to share-dilution timing instead of operational 
issues.

• 4Q26 guidance largely in-line: Management guided for 4Q26 revenue to be flat or 
slightly up QoQ on traditional seasonality, which is in line with our forecast (up 3% 
QoQ). On margins, management guided for a slight QoQ decline in gross margin, 
mainly due to higher labour cost and depreciation expenses, which is also consistent 
with our forecast. We estimate 4Q26 gross margin at 44.0%, down 1.4ppt QoQ.

• Solid demand for 2027: For 2027, we expect Ardentec to continue benefiting from the 
AI-driven growth trend, supported by (1) robust wafer sorting outsourcing demand from 
tier-1 foundry customers and (2) strong AI networking demand from a major U.S. 
networking customer. The company is currently engaged in several co-packaged optics 
(CPO) projects with leading U.S. industry players, which could provide it long term 
opportunities and a larger addressable market.

### Page 1

Summary: We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show that 
was held on 20-22 October. Below, we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key 
company takeaways. Overall, we remain positive on Taiwan glass fibre, CCL, PCB, and 
substrate vendors, supported by upbeat company commentary. On pricing, we expect 
low-end CCL and E-glass price increases to be larger and more sustained than previously 
anticipated. High-end CCL vendor commentary suggests plans for more meaningful price 
increases from 2H26 to maintain their products’ relative profitability amid sharp increases 
in low-end CCL prices. We also expect prices for T-glass, ABF, CCL, and substrates to 
continue rising. On the CCL technology roadmap, we favour Q-glass combined with 
hydrocarbon resin (HC) over PTFE-based alternatives as the longer-term direction. Lastly, 
we provide our view on the CCL market competition landscape change after seeing 
Nan Ya Plastics targeting the high-end market more aggressively. We also provide some of 
our key highlights from related companies within the Taiwan PCB supply chain. EMC (2383 
TT, TWD4,750, Buy [1]), ITEQ (6213 TT, TWD746, Buy [1]), Kinsus (3189 TT, TWD941, 
Buy [1]) and NYPCB (8046 TT, TWD1,255, Buy [1]) are our top ideas within our coverage.

We attended the 2026 Taiwan Printed Circuit Association (TPCA) Show last week. Below, 
we share our read-throughs from booth visits and key company takeaways.

Whether to adopt PTFE emerged as a key point of debate at the show.  
• During the TPCA show, Taiwan Union Technology (TUC; 6274 TT, TWD1,280, Buy [1]) 
unveiled its new M10 (Hydrocarbon base) and M11 (PTFE base) materials with glass-
fabric (Low-Dk2, Q-glass) and glass-fabric-free versions. Nan Ya Plastic (1303 TT, not 
rated) also unveiled its M11-grade material (ie, NPG-A99) at the show. Based on our 
understanding, NPG-A99 will continue to a hydrocarbon resin (HC) base, likely 
combined with Low-Dk2, to enhance CCL performance for high frequency and high-
speed signal transmission requirements. However, Elite Material (EMC; 2383 TT, 
TWD4,750, Buy [1]) remains reluctant to enter the PTFE-based CCL market, citing 
limited end application. Also, EMC plans to use advanced Q-glass for its M10 CCL. 
Lastly, we also heard from Panasonic’s booth representative that Panasonic (6752 JP, 
JPY3,687, Neutral [3]; covered by Ayaka Inomata) is pursuing a similar strategy for 
M9/M10 development, leveraging Q-glass instead of PTFE at this stage.

• While Nvidia (NVDA US, USD216, Outperform [2]; covered by Louis Miscioscia) 
appears keen to enhance product performance by adopting PTFE, given its very low 
dissipation factor (Df), PCB manufacturing yields remain a key concern. Accordingly, 
some leading vendors are broadening their material options (including PTFE-based 
CCL) to address potential customer requirements. EMC and Panasonic, however, 
remain firmly committed to a Q-glass-based technology roadmap. Although TUC 
highlighted progress on its PTFE-based M11 at a technology conference during the 
show, we believe it also intends to advance its existing HC resin/Low-Dk2 and HC 
resin/Q-glass roadmap for its next-generation CCL. In our view, its PTFE-based M11 
development is intended to preserve flexibility across potential technology pathways 
rather than replace its existing approach. Nan Ya Plastics’s strategy also appears 
reasonable: it can produce Low-Dk glass in-house but must source Q-glass and PTFE 
externally. This sourcing structure may explain its continued use of Low-Dk2 with HC-
resin instead of PTFE for its next-gen products, including M11.

 Taiwan PCB: Our key takeaways from 2026 TPCA show

Sheng Cheng

### Page 2

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
2

• Conclusion: which technology roadmap will prevail? We believe it is too early to 
identify a clear winner, as the supply chain continues to improve manufacturing yields 
for PTFE-based products. Some vendors remain cautious despite PTFE’s long history in 
the industry, as its thermal shrinkage and relative softness still make adoption in high-
layer-count (HLC) PCBs challenging. Given the current stance of leading global vendors 
EMC, Doosan (000150 KS), and Panasonic, together with EMC’s execution track 
record, we currently favour Q-glass-based solutions without PTFE as the more likely 
direction. Nevertheless, broadening product offerings to address different customer 
requirements should not be viewed negatively, provided vendors have sufficient 
resources to support development across multiple technology pathways.

Low-end CCL and e-glass continue to surprise on the upside.  
• During the show, we also spoke with Kingboard Laminates (1888 HK, not rated). The 
company remained positive on the E-glass outlook, although its booth representative 
provided no numerical guidance for next year following a c.200% price hike in 2026. 
Kingboard attributed the pricing strength to supply constraints, driven largely by AI 
server demand. While we highlighted increased glass yarn supply from Chinese peers in 
2Q/3Q26, the company argued that the weaving machine capacity remains the 
bottleneck. It expects to add only c.500 weaving machines in 2026 and 1.2k in 2027 (vs. 
current installed base of 3.3k). E-glass fabric price increases therefore appear 
supported by limited weaving capacity and producers shifting capacity from lower-end 
glass to higher-end products, including Low-Dk, Low-Dk2, and T-glass. Kingboard also 
noted a similar trend in low-end copper foil, where shortages have recently intensified.

• The read-through from Nan Ya Plastics is similar. The company indicated that its current 
fibreglass allocation between internal consumption and external sales is 45:55, with the 
internal share set to rise gradually as it upgrades its product mix. Its CCL portfolio 
remains concentrated in FR-4 (M2 grade), but it plans to migrate toward M4/M6, 
supported by long-term contract wins with two major hyperscalers seeking to secure 
CCL supply. In 2027, M4/M6 products are expected to account for 60-70% of its mix (4m 
out of 6.5m in capacity), with M2 and below contributing 20%. The remainder would 
comprise high-end CCL, including M7/M8/M9, reflecting recent development progress. 
We therefore expect Nan Ya’s external thin-type E-glass supply to decline as its M4/M6 
mix increases next year.

Competition from Nan Ya Plastics.   
• The current CCL shortage creates favourable conditions for Nan Ya Plastics to expand 
into the high-end CCL supply chain. Whether the company has entered AMZN’s T3/T4 
supply chain is also a key point of market debate. Based on discussions with its booth 
representatives, however, we believe management will prioritise upgrading its M4/M6 
product portfolio before pursuing the highest-end CCL market more aggressively. On the 
positive side, favourable supply-demand dynamics have supported Nan Ya’s transition 
from a low-end supplier toward the mid-to-high-end segment, although high-end CCL 
should still contribute a relatively small share of revenue in 2027. For incumbent 
suppliers, competition is clearly intensifying, but we expect the near-term impact on 
high-end peers to remain limited.

• Our read: We expect the impact on EMC and TUC to remain relatively limited in 2027. 
Competitive pressure could become more meaningful over 2028-2030, although it is too 
early to assess its magnitude.

### Page 3

Taiwan Data Centre Hardware 
27 October 2026 
3

Other key takeaways: 
• Nitto Boseki (3110 JP, JPY2,942, Neutral [3]; covered by Noritsugu Hirakawa) identified 
Taiwan Glass (1802 TT, NR) and Grace Fabric Technology (603256 CH, CNY84.95, 
Outperform [2]; covered by Dennis Ip) as key competitors. Its plan to triple capacity by 
2028 remains largely on track. The company also intends to expand capacity further by 
2030, although the details have yet to be confirmed.

• Resonac (4004 JP, JPY12,915, Outperform [2]; covered by Hidemitsu Umebayashi) 
indicated that customers are requesting ABF substrate core thicknesses of 2,000-
3,000μm, versus its current maximum of 1,600μm, to accommodate larger package 
sizes. Potential technology pathways include adopting glass cores or ceramic 
substrates, or stacking additional core layers to enhance rigidity. Based on discussions 
with booth representatives, substrate makers may move toward multi-core structures, 
which could increase T-glass consumption. However, technical challenges in enabling 
signal transmission between core layers remain a potential barrier that would need to be 
addressed over the next one to two years.

• Mitsui Kinzoku (5706 JP, JPY2,054, Outperform [2]; covered by Shinichiro Ozaki) 
indicated that, based on its technology roadmap, M10-grade CCL combined with its 
HVLP5 copper foil should support 448Gbps SerDes transmission. In the near term, the 
company plans to shift capacity from lower-end to higher-end HVLP products, with a key 
piece of production equipment scheduled for installation in 2028/29. On pricing, its booth 
representative suggested that quarterly increases of more than 10% would be 
appropriate. Regarding copper foil shortages highlighted by other supply-chain 
participants, the representative indicated that mid-tier products, such as HVLP1 and 
HVLP2, could face the most severe supply constraints.

• Unimicron (3037 TT, TWD1,060, Buy [1]) appeared to highlight multi-core structures and 
the embedding of passive components within core layers as key technology trends in 
ABF substrates.

• Zhen Ding Technology (ZDT; 4958 TT, not rated) indicated that its substrate business 
has a plan to list on the Hong Kong Stock Exchange in 1H27 under the name Leading 
Interconnect Semiconductor Technology (LIST). At the show, the company focused 
primarily on thick HDI boards and highlighted two next-generation technology trends: 1) 
sequential lamination (M+N) to improve signal integrity and increase routing density 
within the same footprint; and 2) hybrid HDI/HLC structures, which add HDI layers to 
thick HLC boards (M+N), as discussed earlier.

### Page 1

Faraday

Valuation remains stretched; timely 
revenue delivery to be key watchpoint

Reiterate Rating: UNDERPERFORM | PO: 120.00 TWD | Price: 191.50 TWD

3Q26 sales beat on record MCU mass production revenue  
Faraday’s 3Q26 sales was +11% QoQ, above BofAe/street of -7%/-3% QoQ and its 
guidance of -single-digit% QoQ, mainly driven by strong MP growth (+19.6% QoQ) on 
record-high MCU sales and accelerating ASIC shipments, coupled with continued IP 
revenue strength more than offsetting deferred NRE revenue recognition. 3Q26 GM 
dropped by 270 bps to 42.5% due to product mix, while increased Opex from salary 
adjustments brought 3Q26 EPS to NT$1.07 (BofAe/Street NT$1.27/0.66).  
IP and NRE strength support 4Q26 margin improvement 
4Q26 sales is guided to remain flat QoQ, as growth in IP and NRE revenue offset decline 
in MP revenue on lower MCU shipments following a high base in 3Q26. With sustained 
strength in IP segment underpinned by higher wafer pricing and utilization, along with 
NRE milestone delivery, 4Q26 GM should edge up to 43.7% on better mix, in our view.  
High-end MCU and advanced projects drive 2027 growth  
We now forecast Faraday's 2026/2027 sales to -27%/+36% YoY, supported by healthy 
demand for high-end MCU and gradual ramp in advanced projects. While the design 
change from 4nm to 2nm for a US customer’s ASIC project should bring greater contract 
value, Faraday’s capability to deliver on schedule and achieve timely revenue recognition 
remains key watchpoint. On higher advanced packaging/nodes projects contribution 
through 2027, GM should decline slightly from 44.5% in 2026 to 42.9% in 2027. 
Reiterate Underperform with unchanged NT$120 PO 
We lower our 2026 EPS from NT$4.05 to NT$3.19 on higher Opex and trim 2027-28 
EPS by 3-10% as we remain cautious on the timing of NRE/MP recognition for advanced 
projects given the increased design complexity and constrained OSAT capacity. We 
reiterate Underperform rating with unchanged PO of NT$120 on 17x 2027E P/E. Current 
valuation appears stretched with shares trading at 28x '27 P/E (vs. historical 13-42x).

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Faraday Technology Corp. was established in 1993 and 
listed in 1999. The company is an affiliate of the second 
biggest semiconductor logic foundry (UMC) in Taiwan, and 
has been the main provider to UMC's IP library. Faraday is 
also known for its total solution in ASIC (customized chips) 
which includes chip-design and turnkey mass production, 
on top of its IP offering. The company has a diversified end-
market exposure, including internet of things, factory 
automation, solar, microcontrollers etc.

Investment Rationale 
We have an Underperform rating on Faraday, as we are 
wary of the slower for longer business outlook in mature 
node projects, while its collaboration with non-TSMC 
foundries bears the uncertainty.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Faraday (FDYTF) 
Our PO NT$120 is derived from 17x 2027E P/E, which is at low-to-mid-end of the 
company's historical valuation range. In our view, the multiple is backed by gross margin 
expansion from 27% in 2025 to over 40% in 2026/27 while revenue would slow down 
from a peak in 2025.

Upside risks: (1) faster-than-expected progress in new businesses including advanced 
nodes and advanced packaging, and (2) a better margin profile thanks to more stringent 
opex and enhanced value-add to customers

Downside risks: (1) downturn in foundry business cycle, given that the firm adopts a 
cost-plus model for turnkey (mass production) business, and (2) slower qualification and 
ramp-up of the yield rate for advanced packaging business

ab 27 October 2026
Global Research
First Read
Powertech Technology
Subsidiaries drive stronger revenue/OP growth;
FOPLP expansion back on track
Q326 results broadly in-line despite better GM
Q326 EPS of NT$3.54 was broadly in-line with UBSe/consensus as stronger  revenue and
better GM were offset by higher tax and minority interests. Q326 sales rose 9.6% QoQ
to NT$25.3 bn on robust DRAM/eSSD demand and price hikes, ahead of UBSe/Street's
+5-6% QoQ.  GM expanded by 2.2ppt QoQ to 24.0%, supported by better pricing,

higher loadings and more favourable  mix. By business, higher GM testing  increased by
1ppt QoQ to 24% of total sales, while lower margin SiP/module declined 2ppt QoQ to
9%, and packaging  increased 1ppt QoQ to 67%. By entity, we estimate 43%-owned
Greatek  and 61%-owned Teraprobe  each delivered mid-teens % QoQ revenue growth
in Q326, versus  only mid-single % growth at PTI standalone. The stronger contribution
from subsidiaries, however, resulted in higher effective tax rate and minority interests.

Near-term outlook  remains constructive
Management remains constructive on the near-term outlook, supported by AI/cloud
investment cycle, continued memory supply constraint, and PTI's ongoing diversification
into non-memory segments. Q426F UTRs are expected to remain elevated following
over-90% levels in Q3, while ASPs should continue to increase as customers absorb
higher raw material costs. PTI is also expanding its exposure to logic Flip-Chip BGA, while

premium-segment automotive demand remains stable. Nevertheless, consumer
electronics demand is showing some weakness amid higher memory pricing. We
forecast Q426 sales  to grow 4% QoQ and  GM to expand  0.8ppt QoQ to 24.8%.
Looking into 2027E, we believe  GM to remain in the mid-20% range, as continued
memory supply tightness and  better mix should largely offset higher  depreciation.
FOPLP expansion back on track
PTI indicated its FOPLP expansion remains on track for mass production in 2027, with the

equipment delivery schedule having improved. It sees robust customer demand  with
notable increases in New Product Introduction (NPI). We continue to model FOPLP
capacity of 3K substrates/month in 2026E, rising to 6K by mid-2027E in Taiwan, with
panel substrate size of 510 mm x 515 mm. Importantly, PTI  aims to serve end-customers
directly and capture greater value-add, rather than operate  as an outsourcing partner to
leading wafer foundry. Its JV with Broadcom remains  under review by Taiwan's MOEA

Investment Commission with management expecting a decision in the  near-term.
Valuation: Raise PT to NT$280, retain NEUTRAL
We revise our 2026/27/28E EPS by 3%/9%/7% on better sales/GM, offset by higher tax
and minority interest from subsidiaries. We raise PT to NT$280 (from NT$260) with the
same 17x 2027E PE. While near-term outlook has improved we believe further upside
will depend on the execution of the  FOPLP ramp and the sustainability of pricing

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

信邦(3023 TT)  
台灣股市︱電子中游 
投 資 速 報 
買進-首次 目標價 NT$373 (潛在報酬+35.6%) 2026-10-28

半導體設備連接線需求 強勁  
◼ 結 論 與 建 議 
基 於 以 下 理 由 ， 研 究 部 給 予 信 邦 逢 低 買 進 評 等  
1. 1~3 Q26 營 收 達 271.83 億 元 ， 年 增 15.24% ， 3~9 月 營 收 連 增 7 個月 ，
表 現 優 於 市 場 預 期 。  
2. 工 業 （ 半 導 體 設 備 、 自 動 化 ） 、 醫 療 及 車 用 連 接 線 為 三 大 長 期 成 長 引 擎 。  
3. 公 司 半 導 體 設 備 從 線 束 升 級 至 機 櫃 組 裝 大 幅 提 升 附 加 價 值 與 能 見 度；汽 車
業 務 受 惠 電 動 化 與 控 制 模 組 放 量；醫 療 則 受 惠 中 國 市 場 回 溫 可 提 供 近 兩
年 明 確 動 能，人 形 機 器 人 則 為 2028 年 後 的 爆 發 潛 力， 預估 2027 年 EPS  
17.78 元 PE 約 15. 5X， 建 議 買 進 ， 目 標 價 為 373 元 (21* 202 7 年 E P S )。

◼ 重點摘要  
公 司 簡 介  
公司是台灣領先的電子零件整合設計與製造服務商，專注於客製化線束
（ Cable Assembly ） 、 精 密 連 接 器 組 件 及 系 統 整 合 解 決 方 案 。 核 心 業 務 以 提
供從設計、打樣、製造到組裝的完整解決方案。產品以連接線組為主（約
78% ） ， 連 接 器 及 零 組 件 約22% 。 代 理 Hirose 等 國 際 大 廠 連 接 器 。 客 戶 涵 蓋
全球前十大風機廠商中的 8 家、前 十 大 醫 療 成 像 設 備 商 中 的 6 家、前 二 十 大
工業設備商中的 9 家、前十大汽車商中的 6 家等。 2025 年產品應用占比大
致為工業約 31% 、通訊 24% 、 綠 能 20% 、 汽 車 / 航太 16% 、 醫 療 9% （實際
會 隨 年 度 波 動 ） 。 銷 售 地 區 以 中 國 大 陸 為 主（ 約53% ） ， 其 次 為 美 國 、 台 灣 、
歐洲等。積極擴充半導體設備機櫃組裝產能及商用無人機相關產品。

全球半導體設備市場規模快速成長  
根 據 市 調 機 構 數 據 顯 示 ， 全 球 前 段 製 程 設 備 （ Waf er Fa b Equipmen t, 
WFE ）， 2025 年 銷 售 額 創 下 1,169 億 美 元 新 高 ， 預 估 2026 年將再成長
23.1% ，達 1,439 億 美 元 。 此 數 字 較 SEMI 先 前 於 2025 年 底 所 公 布 的 預 測
大 幅 上 修 ， 主 要 受 惠 於 先 進 記 憶 體 、 尤 其 是 HBM 相關 DRAM 技 術 ， 以 及
先 進 邏 輯 應 用 投 資 持 續 增 加 。 隨 著 製 造 商 因 應 AI 應 用 需 求 ， 加 速 擴 充 產 能
及 推 進 技 術 升 級，預 估 2027 年及 2028 年 將 分 別 成 長 21.8% 及 14.1% ，2028
年 市 場 規 模 可 望 突 破 2,000 億 美 元 。  
圖 二 、 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 市 場 預 測

資 料 來 源 ： SEMI 、 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

工業應用為 最 強 成 長 動 能  
1~3Q26 營 收 占 比 37% ， YOY+38.9% ， 主 要 受 惠 於 全 球 半 導 體 設 備 需 求 擴
張 及 智 能 製 造 自 動 化 升 級 潮 。 內 部 結 構 佔 比 包括 倉 儲 物 流 自 動 化 近 40% 、
半 導 體 設 備 佔 30 ~35% 、 運 輸 / 建設 設 備 線 束 佔 10% 、 能 源 管 理 類 佔 1 0% 。
動能 主 要 來 自 半 導 體 設 備 機 內 / 機 外 訊 號 與 電 源 線 束 組 裝 以 及 倉 儲 物 流 自
動 化 需 求 增 加 ， 27F 營收 YOY+20% 。  
a. 半 導 體 設 備 : 為 最 大 亮 點 ， 從 傳 統 線 束 升 級 至 整 機 機 櫃 組 裝 與 系 統 整 合 。
提 供 機 內 ／ 機 外 訊 號 線 與 電 源 線，並 開 始 整 合 其 他 零 組 件 做 成 小 型 機 櫃
／ 模 組 給 客 戶 。  
✓ 供 應 鏈 地 位 ： 公司 為 全 球 半 導 體 前 段 與 後 段 設 備 大 廠 （ 如  AS ML 、應
用 材 料 等 ） 的 重 要 高 度 客 製 化 線 束 及 系 統 整 合 供 應 商 。  
✓ 應 用 領 域 ： 先 進 節 點 製 造 中 的 光 刻 （ 微 影 ） 、 蝕 刻 和 沉 積 設 備 、 精密
製 程 設 備 中 的 線 束 系 統 、 機 櫃 半 成 品 組 裝 。  
✓ 訂 單 與 產 能 狀 況： 受 產 品 銷 量 增 長 、 複 雜 性 提 升 及 新 客 戶 訂 單 需 求 驅
動，公 司 目 前 通 過 加 班 和 增 加 第 二 班 次 滿 足 產 能 需 求 ；為 進 一 步 擴
充 產 能 ， 斥 資 逾 50 億 元 在 苗 栗 銅 鑼 科 學 園 區 廠 房 擴 建 產 能 ， 主 要
目 的 即 是 用 於 擴 充 半 導 體 設 備 高 階 模 組 與 線 束 生 產 量 能 。 計 劃 於
2Q27 年 正 式 投 入 運 營 ， 預計 增 長 趨 勢 將 持 續 到 2030 年 。

人 、 智 慧 安 全 背 心 形 成 完 整 智 慧 物 流 生 態 系 。  
表 二 、 智 慧 倉 儲 無 人 化 生 態 系 統

資 料 來 源 ： 康 和 投 顧 整 理 ， 2026/10

醫 療 及 車 用 產 品 穩 定 成 長  
醫療 ： 1~3Q26 營 收 佔 比 9.8% ， YOY+28.4% ， 以 病 人 監 護 與 影 像 診 斷 設 備
內 部 之 傳 輸 線 束 為 主 ( 中系 ) ， GPM> 平 均 ， 27F 營收 YOY+10~15% 。  
a. 產品 : 專 注 於 高 階 大 型 醫 療 設 備 線 束 ， 包 括  CT （ 電 腦 斷 層 ） 、 MRI （ 核 磁
共 振 ） 、 超 音 波 診 斷 設 備  及  加 護 病 房 （ ICU ） 病 人 監 護 系 統 。  
b. 技 術 壁 壘 ： 醫 療 線 束 要 求 極 高 的 生 物 相 容 性 、 抗 干 擾 性 （ EMI ） 與 長 期 高
穩 定 度 ， 認 證 時 間 長 達 2~3 年 以 上 ， 屬 於 「 高 客 製 化 、 高 利 基 」 市 場 。 
c. 成 長 動 能 : 受 惠 中 國 客 戶 加 大 出 口 與 海 外 專 案 進 入 量 產 ， 2026 年 成 長 明
顯 ， 毛 利 率 優 於 平 均 ， 2027 年 預 估 成 長 10 – 15% ， 提 供 穩 定 貢 獻 與 產
品 組 合 優 化 效 果 。  
d. 客戶 : 包括 GE Health car e 、Ph ilips 及 S iemens Healthin eers ， 產 品 滲 透
率 持 續 提 升 。  
車用 :1~3Q26 營 收 占 比 16.5% ， YOY+25.5% ， 充 電 線 （ 電 動 車 與 充 電 樁 ）
及 一 般 汽 車 電 子 訊 號 線（ 胎 壓 偵 測 、 360 度 環 景 、 倒 車 雷 達 等 ） 表 現 持 穩 ，
主 要 成 長 動能 來 自 控 制 器 ， 整 合 5 個 E CU 功 能 （ 含 引 擎 控 制 、 熱 管 理 、 電
池管理、訊號控 制等），提升運算效率。主要客戶為上汽， 27F 營收
YOY+10~15% 。  
a. 產品 : 包 括 充 電 線（ 電 動 車 與 充 電 樁 ）； 一 般 汽 車 電 子 訊 號 線（ 胎 壓 偵 測 、
360 度 環 景 、 倒 車 雷 達 等 ） ； PDCU （ Power Domain Con tr ol Un it ） 控 制
器： PCB 組 裝 產 品 ， 將 過 去 約 5 個 ECU 功 能 （ 引 擎 控 制 、 熱 管 理 、 電 池 管
理 、 訊 號 控 制 等 ） 整 合 成 一 顆 「 大 控 制 器 」 ， 為 26~27 年 主 要 成 長 來 源 。  
b. 主要出貨給中國油電混合車客戶（以上海汽車體系為主，部分品牌有出
口 ） ， 並 擴 展 至 日 韓 等 市 場 。  
c. 展望 : 純 電 動 車 充 電 相 關 今 年 大 致 持 平，成 長 主 要 來 自 PD CU 控 制 器。2027
年 預 估 成 長 10 – 15% 。 長 期 受 惠 電 動 化 與 高 電 壓 ／ 大 電 流 解 決 方 案 需 求 。

體 機 櫃 與 線 束 ） 。

評等說明  
1)  強力 買進 / Str on g Bu y ： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正 向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 大 於 50 % 
2)  買進 / Bu y： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正 向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 大 於 25%  
3)  逢低買進 /Tr a din g Bu y ： 我 們 對 該 評 價 個 股 未 來 展 望 正向 ， 並 預 估 於 未 來 12 個 月 投 資 期 間 ， 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 小 於 25%  
4)  未評等 / N ot Ra te d ： 至 目 前 我 們 沒 有 足 夠 基 本 資 料 判 斷 該 公 司 評 等 ； 或 此 期 間 康 和 與 該 公 司 有 特 定 交 易 ， 基 於 規 範 或 康
和 政 策 ， 限 制 予 該 公 司 評 等

免責宣言 :  
本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 康 和 投 資 顧 問 股 份 有 限 公 司 版 權 所 有 ， 禁 止 任 何 形 式 之 抄 襲 、 引 用 或 轉 載 。 本 研 究 報 告 所 載 內 容 ， 純 屬
研 究 性 質 ， 所 載 述 意 見 可 隨 時 予 以 更 改 或 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 ， 僅 提 供 康 和 集 團 內 部 同 仁 及 客 戶 參 考 ， 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何
意 見 ， 不 構 成 ( 且 不 應 解 釋 為 ) 在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 劵 或 其 他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約 、 招 攬 、 邀 請 、 宣 傳 、 誘 使 ，
或 任 何 不 論 種 類 或 形 式 之 表 示 、 建 議 或 推 薦 買 賣 本 研 究 報 告 所 述 的 任 何 證 劵 。 報 告 內 容 所 提 及 之 各 項 業 務 、 財 務 等 相 關 資
訊 、 意 見 及 預 估 ， 係 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資 料 來 源 ， 既 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 做 出 任
何 陳 述 或 保 證 ， 且 報 告 係 基 於 特 定 日 期 所 做 之 判 斷 ， 有 其 時 效 性 限 制 ， 如 有 變 更 ， 本 公 司 將 不 做 預 告 或 更 新 。 對 於 本 投 資
報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 劵 、 投 資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 於 決 策 時 應 審 慎 衡 量 本 身 風 險 ， 並 就
投 資 結 果 自 行 負 責 。

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

### Page 1

2026년 10월 29일 
기업분석

SK하이닉스 (000660) 
높아지는 이익의 눈높이, 달라지는 메모리 사이클

Buy(유지) 
목표주가 3,150,000원, 현재 주가(10/28): 1,487,000원 
▶ Analyst 박준영 jyp94@hanwha.com 3772-7481 / RA 김나우 now.kim@hanwha.com 3772-7710

3Q26 Review: HBM4 출하 확대와 예상보다 강한 가격 상승 
분기 매출액 104.6조원(QoQ +32%, YoY +328%), 영업이익 83.3조원(QoQ +38%, YoY +632%)을 기록하며 시장 기대
치를 상회함. 영업이익률은 전분기 대비 약 3%p 상승한 80%로 사상 최대 분기 실적을 달성함. 특히 DRAM ASP가 전
분기 대비 20% 후반 상승하며 당사 기존 예상치인 +23%를 상회한 점에 주목할 필요가 있음. NAND ASP 역시 20% 
중반 상승하며 예상보다 강한 가격 흐름을 나타냄. DRAM ASP 상승에는 기존 DRAM의 가격 인상과 함께 3분기부터 
본격화된 HBM4 양산 및 출하 확대에 따른 제품 믹스 개선 효과가 반영됨. 서버 DDR5 판매량 역시 전분기 대비 40% 
증가하며 실적 성장을 뒷받침함. 특히 HBM4는 기존 HBM3E 대비 높은 기술적 난도를 반영한 가격으로 공급되고 있어 
향후 출하 비중 확대에 따른 추가적인 ASP 상승 효과도 기대됨. 
이번 실적에서 중요한 부분은 예상보다 높은 가격 상승폭이 2027년 실적 추정치의 상향 가능성을 높이고 있다는 점임. 3
분기 DRAM ASP가 기존 추정치를 상회한 가운데 4분기에도 HBM4 출하 확대와 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 이어질 
것으로 예상되기 때문임. 동사는 4분기 DRAM 출하량이 전분기 대비 20% 중반 증가할 것으로 전망했으며, HBM4 출
하량이 HBM3E를 넘어 주력 제품으로 자리 잡을 것으로 예상함. 특히 HBM4의 양산 수율이 당초 목표를 상회하고 있
으며 높은 고객 수요에 대응하기 위해 생산성 극대화에 집중하고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. HBM4 출하 확대에 따른 믹스 
개선과 기존 DRAM 가격 상승이 동시에 진행되는 가운데 HBM과 일반 DRAM의 수익성 격차 역시 유의미하게 축소
될 것으로 예상됨. 이는 특정 제품에 대한 수익성 의존도가 낮아지면서 DRAM 사업 전반의 이익 체력이 강화되고 있음
을 의미함. 메모리 산업의 높은 영업 레버리지를 고려하면 예상보다 높은 ASP와 출하량 증가는 2027년 이익 추정치의 
추가 상향으로 연결될 가능성이 높다고 판단함.

공급 부족의 장기화와 LTA 확대, 메모리 산업의 이익 구조 변화 
이번 컨퍼런스콜에서 확인된 또 다른 중요한 변화는 공급 부족의 장기화와 장기공급계약(L TA) 확대임. 동사는 첨단 공정
의 기술적 난도 상승과 신규 생산시설 구축에 소요되는 시간을 고려할 때 메모리 공급이 수요를 충족하지 못하는 환경이 
2030년까지 지속될 것으로 전망함. 특히 글로벌 주요 고객사 14곳과 장기공급계약을 체결했으며, 2027~2028년 전체 생
산능력의 50% 이상에 해당하는 물량을 확보함. HBM의 경우 2029년까지의 물량이 이미 판매 확정된 상황으로 중장기 
수요 가시성이 상당히 높아졌다고 판단함. 주목할 부분은 L TA가 단순한 공급물량 확보를 넘어 메모리 업체의 사업 구조
와 이익 안정성을 변화시키고 있다는 점임. 계약 가격은 고객 및 제품별로 차이가 있으나 합의된 범위 내에서 시장 상황
을 반영하는 구조로 운영되고 있으며 예치금 등 실질적인 계약 이행력을 높이는 장치도 포함됨. 이는 장기적인 판매물량
을 확보하면서도 업황 상승에 따른 가격 인상 효과를 일정 부분 누릴 수 있다는 점에서 긍정적임.

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

2 
특히 전체 생산능력의 50% 이상을 L TA로 확보하면서 나머지 물량은 시장 상황에 따라 탄력적으로 대응할 수 있다는 점
에 주목할 필요가 있음. 장기계약을 통해 안정적인 매출 기반을 확보하는 동시에 미계약 물량을 활용해 추가적인 가격 
상승에 대응할 수 있기 때문임. 물론 장기계약이 메모리 가격 하락을 완전히 방어하는 것은 아니지만, 과거와 비교해 물
량 가시성과 고객사와의 협상력이 동시에 개선되고 있다는 점은 분명함. 과거 메모리 산업이 높은 이익을 창출하는 국면
에서도 낮은 밸류에이션을 적용 받았던 이유 중 하나는 이익의 지속성에 대한 불확실성이었음. 그러나 장기계약 확대와 
공급 제약의 장기화가 동시에 진행된다면 시장이 평가하는 이익의 지속성 역시 높아질 수 있음. 따라서 향후 주가 상승
은 단순한 EPS 추정치 상향뿐 아니라 이익 변동성 축소에 따른 밸류에이션 재평가가 동시에 진행되는 형태로 나타날 가
능성이 높다고 판단함. 
AI 인프라 투자에 대한 우려 역시 중장기 메모리 수요를 훼손할 가능성은 제한적이라고 판단함. 동사는 주요 하이퍼스케
일러의 클라우드 매출이 전년 대비 30~80% 성장하고 있으며 기업용 AI 솔루션 계약 확대와 클라우드 수주잔고 증가를 
통해 AI 투자의 사업적 성과가 확인되고 있다고 설명함. 특히 초기 AI 투자가 소수 빅테크에 집중되었던 것과 달리 최근
에는 일반기업의 자체 인프라 구축, 소버린 AI, 온프레미스 등으로 투자 주체가 다변화되고 있다는 점도 긍정적임. 특정 
사업자의 투자 속도 조절이 전체 AI 인프라 수요에 미치는 영향은 과거 대비 완화될 수 있기 때문임. AI 투자에 대한 우
려가 지속되는 상황에서도 실제 메모리 수요와 고객사들의 장기계약 확대가 확인되고 있다는 점에서 메모리 업황의 구조
적 성장에 대한 긍정적인 시각을 유지함.

HBM4 경쟁력 강화와 CAPEX 확대, 이익 성장과 주주환원의 선순환 
HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대와 신규 생산능력 확보 역시 2027년 실적 성장의 중요한 기반이 될 전망임. 동사는 M15X
의 생산능력을 연말까지 최대 수준으로 확대할 계획이며 HBM4의 양산 수율 역시 당초 목표를 상회하는 등 안정적인 
생산체계를 확보하고 있음. 특히 HBM 수요가 공급능력의 두 배를 상회하는 상황에서 고객사들의 높은 선호가 이어지고 
있다는 점은 경쟁력 유지에 긍정적임. 최근 일부 고객사들이 HBM 적층 단수를 낮추는 방안을 검토하고 있으나 이는 공
급 부족 상황에서 제한된 물량을 효율적으로 활용하기 위한 전략으로 판단됨. 적층 단수 조정이 전체 HBM 수요 감소로 
직결되는 것은 아니며 오히려 후공정 생산능력에 대한 요구가 확대될 가능성도 존재함. HBM4E와 커스텀 HBM 개발 
역시 고객사 일정에 맞춰 진행되고 있어 차세대 제품에서도 경쟁력을 유지할 것으로 기대됨. 
한편 2027년 CAPEX는 선단 공정 전환과 용인 클러스터를 비롯한 국내외 생산 인프라 확충으로 전년 대비 증가할 전망
임. 다만 CAPEX 확대를 과거와 같은 공급 과잉의 신호로 해석할 필요는 없다고 판단함. 선단 공정의 기술적 난도 상승
과 고부가 제품 비중 확대에 따라 투자금액 증가가 실제 생산량 증가로 정비례하지 않기 때문임. 특히 동사는 신규 팹 
건설 일정을 앞당기면서도 클린룸은 순차적으로 개방하고 실제 생산능력 확대는 최종 수요와 수익성을 고려해 단계적으
로 진행할 계획임. 고객사의 투자 계획뿐 아니라 재무건전성과 자금조달 여력, 공급망 준비 상황까지 점검해 CAPEX 집
행에 반영한다는 점도 긍정적임. 이는 AI 인프라 투자 확대에 대응하면서도 과거 메모리 산업의 공급 과잉 위험을 최소
화하기 위한 전략으로 판단함. 
동사는 이에 더해 2026년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원 규모의 주주환원 계획을 발표했음. 현금배당 30조원 이상, 자사주 
매입 및 소각 60조원 이상으로 구성되며, 기존에 약속했던 잉여현금흐름(FCF)의 50% 이상을 주주에게 환원한다는 원칙
을 구체화한 것임. 지난 8월 발표한 40조원 규모의 자사주 매입은 10월 21일 완료됐으며, 해당 주식은 향후 소각될 예정
임. 이번 3분기 이사회에서는 현금배당 14조원과 추가 자사주 매입 20조원을 결의했으며, 자사주 매입액 중 10조원은 전
량 소각하고 나머지 10조원은 임직원 보상에 활용할 계획임. 임직원 보상용 자사주는 주주환원 규모에서 제외됨. 특히 
향후에도 자사주 매입을 지속할 계획이라는 점에서 주주환원 정책의 지속성이 강화됐다는 판단임.

### Page 3

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

3 
3분기 주당 배당금은 2만원으로, 실적 개선을 반영한 일반배당 1만원과 키옥시아 투자수익을 활용한 특별배당 1만원으
로 구성됨. 총 배당 규모는 약 14조원이며 회사는 올해 실현한 키옥시아 투자수익의 대부분을 3분기 및 연말 특별배당을 
통해 주주에게 환원할 계획임. 이번 결정은 메모리 가격 상승과 고부가 제품 확대에 따른 현금창출력 개선이 실질적인 
주주환원 확대로 연결되고 있음을 보여줌. 특히 2027년 설비투자 확대가 예상되는 상황에서도 대규모 현금배당과 자사
주 소각을 병행한다는 점은 재무적 여력에 대한 자신감을 반영하는 것으로 판단됨. 향후에도 안정적인 실적 성장과 FCF 
확대가 이어질 경우 주주환원의 지속 가능성이 높아질 전망이며 이는 과거 대비 높아진 이익 창출력과 함께 중장기 밸류
에이션 재평가를 뒷받침하는 요인으로 작용할 것으로 예상됨. 
결국 이번 실적발표에서 확인된 핵심은 예상보다 강한 메모리 가격 상승과 HBM4의 본격적인 출하 확대, 그리고 장기계
약을 통한 이익 가시성 개선임. 특히 HBM4의 높은 가격과 안정적인 양산 수율, 기존 DRAM의 가격 상승이 동시에 확
인되면서 2027년 이익 추정치의 추가 상향 가능성이 높아졌다고 판단함. 여기에 L TA 확대에 따른 중장기 이익 안정성 
개선과 대규모 주주환원까지 더해지고 있음. 실적 추정치 상향과 이익의 지속성 개선이 동시에 진행되고 있다는 점에서 
긍정적인 투자 시각을 유지함.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10 월 29 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성됐음을 확인합니다. 
본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 자료를 
기관투자자 또는 제3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (박준영, 김나우) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 5

SK하이닉스 (000660) [한화리서치]

5 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

유틸리티업종

◼ 목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적, 소규모 SMR 수주 등은 긍정적 
◼ 구체적인 미국 원전 사업개발 시작, 2H27 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
◼ 2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조

목표주가 85,000원(하향). 3분기 실적 양호, 소규모 SMR 수주는 긍정적 
투자의견 ‘매수’ 유지, 목표주가 85,000원으로 하향. 이제는 성장 스토리보다
는 실질적인 원전 수주 확대가 필요한 상황. 의미 있는 수준의 원전 수주 확
대는 2H27부터 본격화될 가능성 존재. 다만, 단기적인 원전 수주 모멘텀 부
족 등을 감안해 6개월 목표주가를 현실화했음 
1) 3Q26 연결기준 영업이익 2,612억원(+91%, 이하 YoY), 에너빌리티 부문 
영업이익 1,211억원(+179%) 등 양호한 실적 기록. 이는 에너빌리티의 가스
터빈, 원전 기자재 매출 증가 등에 기인, 2) 10월 29일 NuScale과 1개 모듈 
주기기 공급 계약을 체결했으며, 4Q26 X-energy와 4개 모듈의 일부 장납기 
품목/소재 수주가 예상되는 등 2Q26 ~ 3Q26 TerraPower와의 일부 품목 계
약에 이어 개별 건의 규모는 작지만 다수의 SMR 관련 기자재 수주가 늘어나
는 점은 긍정적. 3) 최근 폴란드 AP1000 3기의 선발주 장납기 품목 확정(폴
란드 공급업체의 대상 생산 품목 확정, 원자로 압력용기 및 증기발생기는 미
확정)되었으며, 미국 원전 프로젝트가 구체화되기 시작. 4) 향후 AP1000향 
공급 기자재 품목 확대 가능성도 존재. 이는 2H27 본격적인 원전 수주금액 
확대로 이어질 것으로 기대

미국 원전은 구체적 사업개발 시작, 2H27 이후 장납기품목 선발주 기대 
2026년 6월 DOE의 ‘원전 공급망 금융지원 사업 발표(AP1000 10기)’, 8월 
TVA의 ‘2026 IRP’에 2040년까지 최대 원전 5.4GW 승인, 9월 한국과 미국 
상무부 간의 원전 투자 8기 MOU 체결 등을 통해 미국 원전 건설 재개 정책 
및 제도 개편이 구체화되고 강화되었음. 미국 원전 시장은 4Q26 ~ 2027년 
구체적인 사업개발 단계를 거쳐(부지 선정, 원전 운영 SPC 설립, 금융계약체
결, COL 신청 등), 2H27 ~ 2028년 장납기품목 선발주, 2028 ~ 2029년 건
설 계약 단계 진입 예상. 동사는 4Q26 미국 및 국내 가스터빈, 국내 공공 해
상풍력 프로젝트용 터빈, 폴란드 AP1000 주기기 등 미국 원전 이외 분야에
서의 수주 확대 예상. 현재 Rolls-Royce 등과도 SMR 주기기 공급 관련 논의 
중이며, NuScale SMR은 2027년 PPA 체결 시, COL 및 FID 이전 SMR 모듈 
11기(4Q26 1기 계약)의 수주가 가능할 것으로 기대

2027년 체코 원전 시공 입찰 재시도, 시공능력의 중요성 강조 
대우건설·두산에너빌리티 컨소시엄의 체코 원전 시공 계약 협상은 종료되었
지만, 2027년 7월 신규 시공사와 계약 예정. 동사는 입찰에 다시 참여할 예
정. 이번 이슈의 핵심 쟁점은 이미 체코 측과 공사비총액(Lump-sum) 계약을 
맺은 한수원이 시공사와 총액계약을 맺기를 원한 것임. 이번 이슈로 체코 두
코바니 원전 EPC 프로젝트 수익성에 대한 우려가 발생할 수 있지만, 실제 건
설 과정에서 추가 비용이 공사비 계약금액(예비비 포함)을 초과할지는 예단하
기 어려움. 하도급 관리, 공기 준수 등 시공능력의 중요성이 강조되는 상황. 
동사는 3년 전부터 체코에 시공 인력 상주하며 시공 환경 자료 확보, 초기 인
허가, 설계 업무 관여해 왔으며, 주기기 공급사로서 기자재 특성을 활용해 설
치까지 수행 가능한 경쟁력 등을 바탕으로 성공적 수주를 목표하고 있음

표 4. DOE의 미국 대형원전 공급망 금융지원 사업의 대상 장납기품목(LLI; Long-Lead Items) 
 품목 설명  대출과 관련된 확인 근거 
원자로압력용기 
(RPV; Reactor Pressure Vessel) 
핵연료와 노심을 수용하고 원자로 냉각재의 압력을 유지하는 
용기. 앞서 설명한 격납용기와 구분되는 원자로 주기기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
증기발생기 
(SG; Steam Generator) 
원자로에서 나온 열을 전달받아 터빈을 돌릴 증기를 만드는 대
형 열교환기 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
사전제작 구조 모듈 
(PSM; Prefabricated Structural Modules) 
원전 건물의 벽체·바닥 등 구조물을 모듈로 미리 제작하는 방
식의 구성품. AP1000에는 강판과 콘크리트로 구성하는 구조 
모듈이 사용됨 
DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23. 다만, 미국 대
출자료에 개별 모듈 코드까지 열거되지는 않음 
원자로냉각재펌프 
(RCP; Reactor Coolant Pump) 
원자로 냉각재를 순환시켜 원자로에서 발생한 열을 증기발생
기로 전달하는 펌프 DOE 공식 설명자료 — 2026.06.23 
격납용기 
(CV; Containment Vessel) 
원자로압력용기와 주요 설비를 둘러싸며 방사성 물질의 외부 
방출을 억제하는 대형 강철 밀폐 구조물 
DOE EDF 담당자의 직접 발언에서 확인 — 
2026.10.20 공개 인터뷰 
자료: DOE, 대신증권 Research Center

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

Executive summary
雖然⾕歌雲及⾃研晶⽚ TPUs 的營收，獲利及資本⽀出營收及獲利回報率都在
加速，但因為營收佔⽐太低，加上龐⼤的舊業務不僅營收增速緩慢外⽽且還低
於市場預期，這讓整體營收無法提供投資⼈任何驚喜，外加⼀些⾮核⼼業務虧

損擴⼤將⾕歌雲的獲利貢獻全部消耗殆盡，盤後回檔符合預期。以後要持續關
注佔⽐僅整體營收 1/4 的⾕歌雲發展及⾮核⼼業務虧損是否持續擴⼤，尤其是
關注⾃研晶⽚ TPU 銷售及外租對獲利的影響。
The rest sucks
雖然⾕歌  3Q26 的服務營收仍有 10.6% 的年增，但⽐起  Google Cloud 倍增
的營收明顯不⾜，⽽其中不管是搜尋引擎， Youtube 油管業務都低於預期近
2% ，服務業務營業利潤率也低於預期 1 個百分點，這讓服務業務營業利潤⽐市
場預期低了近 3% 。
除此以外，佔⽐不⼤的其他業務  Other Bets ，營業虧損持續擴⼤，從去年的
同期的 14 億美元營業虧損，擴⼤⾄  3Q26 的 20 億美元的營業虧損（ 19% 虧損
⾼於彭博社分析師預期）；加上⾕歌將發⽣在⺟公司的  AI 研發費⽤獨⽴列表
（其中包括員⼯激勵費⽤，，營業虧損從去年同期的 45 億美元，擴⼤⾄  3Q26
的 77 億美元（ 40% 虧損⾼於分析師預期），最後加上⼀筆近 82 億美元的⾮經常

性業外費⽤，這三項費⽤的加總將  Google Cloud 的獲利貢獻完全抵銷殆盡，
投資⼈以後要關注這些虧損是否持續擴⼤。

Document 365: Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read

Source PDF: all report pdf/Regional Roundup_ Coast-to-coast growth From spending to wages, growth is broad-based across the US, though the South is the clear leader in new business applications. 2 min read.pdf
Report source: Bank of America
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

13035502     1

Economy 
Regional Roundup: Coast-to-coast growth 
29 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Bank of America aggregated credit and debit card spending per household is growing at healthy rates across the country. 
Discretionary spending growth has improved markedly relative to the last two years. While the West has led recently and the 
South has lagged somewhat, no region appears to be experiencing a meaningful consumer slowdown. 
• The South has seen softer discretionary retail spending, particularly in home-related durable goods categories. However, the 
region continues to post the strongest after-tax wage growth and has been the largest contributor to growth in new business 
applications nationwide. 
• Business formation is occurring across a wide range of industries, with professional services, retail, healthcare, construction and 
hospitality all accounting for meaningful shares of new business applications. While entrepreneurial activity is broad-based 
across industries nationwide, industry revenue mixes vary considerably by region.

Spending growth is solid across all regions  
Spending growth was strongest in the West in September, while spending growth lagged in the Northeast and South, though not 
by much. Total credit and debit card spending per household increased around 5.6% year-over-year (YoY) in the West, outpacing 
other regions by less than a percentage point, according to Bank of America aggregated card data (Exhibit 1). All regions also 
experienced major improvements in spending growth compared to last year, increasing by over three percentage points in every 
region.   
Spending growth in the Northeast peaked at around 6% YoY in June but remained a solid 4.8% YoY in September. The slowest 
growth was in the South, though spending was still up nearly 4.8% YoY.  
Exhibit 1: Spending growth has mostly improved since the start of the year across all regions 
Total card spending per household by US Census region (three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Discretionary spending growth was also somewhat weaker in the Northeast and South than in the Midwest and West (Exhibit 2). 
However, these two regions have also seen the most improvement in “nice-to-have” spending over the past two years. And here 
spending was also up around 4.8% YoY in both regions, still a very healthy rate of growth.

Durables spending is weighing on discretionary retail spending in the South 
One of the key themes from September in the overall spending data was a broadening in spending growth, particularly in “more 
discretionary” retail categories like clothing, electronics, building materials and non-store/miscellaneous retailers (read more in 
Consumer Checkpoint: Strength in numbers). Bank of America card data shows that this improvement is occurring across all 
regions.  
However, the South lags, though this is mostly due to declines in spending on home-related durables in, for instance, home 
improvement stores and at furniture retailers. In our view, this could reflect some slowdown in domestic migration to the Sun 
Belt and a corresponding easing in home/moving-related spending (read more in On the move: Renovation over relocation?) 
Exhibit 3: The Midwest and Northeast have seen a higher share of discretionary retail categories with spending growth when compared to the 
South and West 
Diffusion index across “more discretionary” retail categories by spending across US Census regions (monthly, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

The South leads in after-tax wage and business application growth 
One reason we are not overly concerned about consumer spending in the South is the strength of after-tax wage growth in the 
region. Bank of America deposit data suggests that after-tax wage growth was strongest there, though only by a slim margin, up 
nearly 4.2% YoY, just ahead of households in the Midwest, and outpacing the West and Northeast by less than half a percentage 
point (Exhibit 4).

Beyond wage income, business ownership offers another potential channel for wealth accumulation. The South has been the 
primary contributor to overall US growth in new business applications so far in 2026, according to US Census Bureau data 
(Exhibit 5).  
While historically only a small share of these applications is likely to become a business employing workers, this surge in 
applications points to a potential pipeline of entrepreneurial activity. For businesses that become established and profitable, 
ownership can provide a pathway to wealth through retained earnings and the accumulation of business equity. Still, the extent 
of that opportunity depends on businesses’ survival, profitability and financing needs (read more in Business Checkpoint: Looking 
into logistics). 
Exhibit 4: After-tax wages are accelerating across all regions, 
though growth was strongest in the South and Midwest

BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE

Business application growth is widespread across regions and industries 
Looking at 2026 business applications by industry, entrepreneurial activity spans multiple industry groupings (Exhibit 6). 
Professional, scientific, technical and other services account for the largest share, followed by retail trade and the grouping 
comprising healthcare, education services and the arts. Additionally, businesses provide a significant source of income for US 
households. In fact, business revenues accounted for nearly 14% of total pre-tax income, according to the US Census’ 2025 
Survey of Consumer Finances.

Combined, this suggests that potential pathways to business ownership extend not only across various households (read more on 
generational shifts in entrepreneurship in July’s A new generation of business formation) but also across many industries, rather 
than a single sector. 
While business applications show the breadth of prospective entrepreneurial activity nationwide, the industry composition of 
existing business revenue offers a regional lens on business activity. Exhibit 7 shows how revenue was distributed across 
industries within regions according to the 2024 Annual Integrated Economic Survey from the US Census Bureau, highlighting the 
extent to which regional economies are concentrated in particular sectors. 
Although wholesale trade was the sector with the largest share of revenue in each region in the US, there is notable variance in 
economic activity from business revenues across the country. For example, finance and insurance accounted for more than 20% 
of revenue in the Northeast, whereas it’s about half that in the West. Also, manufacturing was only 8.5% of revenue in the 
Northeast, and more than 20% in the Midwest. Overall, the data shows a dynamic national business landscape, with broad 
entrepreneurial activity but distinct regional differences in the industries generating revenue.

Methodology  
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a deposit 
account or credit card. Aggregated spend includes total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-peer, cash, and checks.  
Any Small Business payments data represents aggregate spend from Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit 
card. Payroll payments data include channels such as ACH (automated clearing house), bill pay, checks and wire. Bank of America per Small 
Business client data represents activity spending from active Small Business clients with a deposit account or a Small Business credit card and 
at least one transaction in each month. Small businesses in this report include business clients within Bank of America and generally defined as 
under $5mm in annual sales revenue.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Any household consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level. Whenever median household savings and 
checking balances are quoted, the data is based on a fixed cohort of households that had a consumer deposit account (checking and/or savings 
account) for all months from January 2019 through the most current month of data shown.  
Bank of America aggregated credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card 
holders making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs. Value grocers is defined using a combination of MCC code and proprietary methods.  
Lower, middle and higher household income cuts in Bank of America credit and debit card spending per household, and consumer deposit 
account data are based on quantitative estimates of each households’ income. These quantitative estimates are bucketed according to terciles, 
with a third of households placed in each tercile periodically. The lowest tercile represents ‘lower income’, the middle tercile represents ‘middle 
income’ and the highest tercile ‘higher income’. The income thresholds between these terciles will move over time, reflecting any number of 
factors that impact income, including general wage inflation, changes in social security payments and individual households’ income. The 
income and tercile in which a household is categorised are periodically re-assessed.  
Generations, if discussed, are defined as follows: Gen Z, born after 1995; Younger Millennials: born between 1989-1995; Older Millennials: born 
between 1978-1988; Gen Xers: born between 1965-1977; Baby Boomer: 1946-1964; Traditionalists: pre-1946. 
Any reference to card spending per household on gasoline includes all purchases at gasoline stations and might include purchases of non-gas 
items. 
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Fiji’s resilience is being tested...
Fiji’s economy entered 2026 with growth already normalising
from the post-pandemic recovery. Real GDP growth slowed from
3.6% in 2024 to 2.5% in 2025. Foreign reserves started the year

around $3.7bn, banking-system liquidity remained high and the
RBF maintained its Overnight Policy Rate at 0.25%.
Activity so far into 2026 has been somewhat stronger than what
we feared. Visitor arrivals reached 762,352 during January–
September, up 3.7%yr, while construction, investment lending,
cement sales, remittances and private-sector credit remained
firm. Nevertheless, Westpac Fiji has kept its 2026 growth
forecast unchanged at 2.0%. Stronger headline indicators are

not translating one-for-one into real economic growth because
higher prices and intermediate costs are absorbing a growing
share of nominal activity.
The outlook changed materially in March as the Middle East
conflict disrupted global energy markets. Fiji remains heavily
dependent on imported petroleum products, so higher refined-
fuel, freight and insurance costs flowed through transport,
electricity, food distribution and wider business expenses. By

June, motor spirit price reached $3.93/litre and diesel at a record
$4.58/litre, while automotive-fuel sales value rose 24.4%yr in the
June quarter.
As this report goes out, the Middle East conflict remains a
key external downside risk. At least 12 vessels were attacked
in the week ending 16 October and yet it is reported that oil
flows through Hormuz averaged 11.6mn barrels per day to 13
October, about 68% of pre-war levels through alternative and

costly routes. Iran says a seven-day reopening proposal remains
available, but US-Iran negotiations remain unresolved.
Westpac Economics’ most recent scenario analysis “ Persistent
energy pressures have further to run ” indicates that relief will
be gradual. Brent crude is forecast to average US$101/bbl in Q4
2026 before easing through 2027. Refined products are expected
to normalise more slowly because refinery capacity, inventories,
freight and insurance remain constrained. Under Westpac’s

escalation scenario, in a return to open conflict Brent could reach
around US$150/bbl in H1 2027.
Domestic tourism sector demonstrates the nominal-versus-
real growth tension. June-quarter visitor arrivals increased to
287,588, yet earnings fell 4.9%yr to $708.1mn. First-half tourism
earnings declined 1.7%yr despite higher arrivals, even though
hotel takings increased 9.5%yr.
Australia and New Zealand remain critical, together contributing
around 71% of September arrivals. Westpac expects Australian

growth to slow from 2.1% in 2026 to 1.5% in 2027, while New
Zealand strengthens from 1.9% to 2.6%. Tourism therefore
increasingly needs to focus on yield rather than visitor numbers
alone.
Shamal Chand
Senior Economist, Westpac Pacific Economics

WESTPAC PACIFIC ECONOMICS
FIJI ECONOMIC UPDATE
...but the buffers are still doing the heavy lifting...
There are good reasons why growth held up despite these shocks.
Construction work put in place reached $155.9mn in the June
quarter, up 8.6%yr, while investment lending, cement sales and

construction-related imports subsequently remained strong.
Industrial production increased 1.5%yr, although performance
remained uneven across industries.
Inward personal remittances have been particularly impressive,
reaching $1.2bn during January–August, up 32.7%yr. We expect
remittances to reach $1.55bn this year. These inflows, with strong
diaspora support, are helping households absorb higher living
costs while simultaneously supporting Fiji’s external position.

However, the external accounts show the cost of this resilience.
Fiji’s merchandise trade deficit widened to $2.9bn in the first half,
with imports reaching $4.3bn against exports of $1.4bn. Mineral-
fuel imports alone reached around $1.2bn, up 57%yr. Foreign
reserves nevertheless recovered to approximately $4.09bn by
end-September, equivalent to 5.7 months of retained imports , also
supported by Government’s external loan draw-down.
Our in-house import-demand indicator is signalling softer

domestic momentum. Import activity over the three months
to September was down 21.3%yr and fell 39.8% compared
with the preceding three months. The broader six-month
measure declined at a more moderate 16.1% annualised pace.
While volatile, the trend adds to our view that higher prices are
weighing on real activity, despite continued strength across
tourism, remittances, credit and construction.
Headline inflation reached 7.6%yr in August before easing to

6.8% in September, with transport prices up 12.2%yr and food
prices up 7.6%. Our latest forecast has November inflation
around 8.0%yr, average inflation around 4.3% for 2026 and
December (year-end) inflation at 7.4%.
Higher fuel prices are no longer affecting only households.
The shock is moving through freight, electricity, agriculture,
construction materials and wider supply chains. For instance,
we note recent increases in taxi and bus fares. These higher

intermediate costs compress business margins and reduce
household purchasing power.
This also informs our 2027 view. Westpac Fiji has downgraded
its 2027 growth forecast to 2.5%, reflecting the prevailing
effects of the energy crisis, elevated refined-fuel prices and the
broader transmission of higher intermediate costs into next year.
Westpac’s global outlook supports this caution: crude prices are
expected to ease gradually, but refined products could remain

comparatively expensive for longer.
Renewable energy is increasingly important because the fuel
crisis has put a clear economic price on Fiji’s imported-energy
dependence. The faster Fiji prioritises a shift to renewable energy,
the sooner it can establish a more stable energy foundation.
El Niño creates an additional risk through agriculture, hydro
generation and food prices. The buffers remain meaningful, but
they are being asked to work significantly harder.

WESTPAC PACIFIC ECONOMICS
FIJI ECONOMIC UPDATE
...as risks converge
Government finances add another dimension to the outlook.
The FY2025/26 deficit came in at $597.9mn, or 4.3% of GDP,
substantially better than originally budgeted.
Nevertheless, financing requirements are substantial.

Government plans to raise $1.48bn in FY2026/27, comprising
$1.046bn in deficit financing and $436.3mn refinancing. Around
$853.9mn to be domestically sourced and $627.95mn externally,
including a $444mn ADB policy-based loan in November.
Domestic yields therefore deserve closer attention. Fiji’s
government yield curve has steepened considerably, with the
adjustment concentrated beyond 10 years. While the 10-year
yield remained at 3.90%, the 20-year yield rose by 80 basis points

between July 2025 and September 2026 to 5.80%. The 25-year
yield increased even more sharply, climbing from 5.94% to 7.19%
after peaking at 7.25% in mid-2026.
Pressure is also emerging at the short end. The Government’s
20 October Treasury Bill tender offered $50mn across three-,
six- and 12-month maturities. Investors bid for the full amount,
but sought yields of 0.35%, 0.60% and 1.30%, respectively,
above the listed yields of 0.30%, 0.55% and 1.25%. Government

did not accept the higher yields, leaving the entire $50mn offer
unallocated. This suggests investors are increasingly seeking
better returns even on shorter-dated Government securities.
The financial system still provides considerable protection.
Banking-system liquidity stood around $2.28bn at end-
September and the RBF maintained its OPR at 0.25%. In August,
the weighted-average lending rate was 4.51%, the savings-
deposit rate 0.31% and the time-deposit rate 2.01%.

For now, high liquidity limits immediate crowding-out risks.
Higher government yields may encourage banks and institutional
investors to allocate more funds towards government securities.
If liquidity subsequently tightens, deposit rates may need to rise
to retain savings, adding pressure to bank funding costs and
eventually lending rates.
S&P’s decision to maintain Fiji’s B+/B ratings but revise the
outlook from stable to negative reinforces the importance of

debt sustainability, investor confidence and credible medium-
term fiscal consolidation.
Political uncertainty adds another layer, with ongoing public
debate on constitutional amendments and the general election
due by 6 February 2027.
This brings us back to the growth story. We have maintained
2026 growth at 2.0% because resilient tourism, remittances,
construction and investment are being offset by unexpectedly
high inflation and intermediate costs. Our downgrade of 2027

growth to 2.5% recognises that this adjustment will take longer
than previously expected, while fiscal, financial, energy, climate
and political risks increasingly overlap.

WESTPAC PACIFIC ECONOMICS
INFLATION OUTLOOK
Cost pressures move beyond the pump
Inflation has emerged as a key pressure point for Fiji, threatening 
to erode gains from otherwise resilient domestic activity. 
Headline inflation accelerated to 6.8%yr in September, driven by 
essentials: food prices rose 7.6%yr, transport increased 12.2%yr 
and housing and utilities were 4.9% higher. The underlying 
picture remains less severe, with prices declining 0.4%mth in 
September and annual-average inflation at 1.9%. Nevertheless, 
the acceleration suggests the global fuel shock is increasingly 
working its way through the domestic economy.

The import-price pipeline reinforces this view. Import prices 
increased 4.9%qtr and 4.4%yr in the June quarter, including 
a 15.4%yr rise in mineral-product prices as petrol, diesel and 
aviation-fuel costs increased. Export prices rose 6.2%yr, although 
the terms of trade eased 0.6%qtr. Elevated energy costs are 
also spreading beyond the pump. Transport-services producer 
prices increased 2.7%qtr in June, while building-material prices 
rose 1.5%qtr in September, signalling broader pressure across 
distribution, construction and services.

Fuel-price pressures intensified again in October, when regulated 
unleaded prices in Viti Levu increased to $3.15/litre and diesel 
rose by 21 cents to $3.41/litre. Prices nevertheless remain below 
their June peaks. Our indicative estimates point to further 
increases in fuel prices over the coming months.

The global outlook offers little immediate relief. Westpac 
Economics expects Brent to average US$101/bbl in Q4 2026 
before easing to US$77/bbl by Q4 2027, but refined fuels are 
expected to ease more slowly as refinery supply remains more 
constrained, while shipping, insurance and inventory pressures 
gradually normalise. Westpac’s Australian forecasts provide 
some regional context, with diesel and petrol expected to 
average 274c/l and 230c/l, respectively, in Q4 before declining 
through 2027.

For Fiji, the distinction between crude and refined-product prices 
is important. Singapore gasoil prices, freight and insurance costs 
are expected to remain elevated and ease only gradually through 
2027, meaning lower Brent prices may not translate immediately 
into cheaper imported fuel. Higher diesel costs feed into freight, 
construction, agriculture and distribution, while aviation-
fuel pressures add to airline operating costs and tourism-
sector margins. Repeated shocks increase the likelihood that 
businesses eventually pass more of these costs on to consumers, 
particularly through food and other essentials.

We consequently expect inflation to remain elevated through 
year-end, rising towards 8.0% in November before easing to 7.4% 
in December, taking annual-average inflation to around 4.3%. 
The concern is increasingly that an imported fuel shock develops 
into broader cost-push inflation into next year. The squeeze on 
real purchasing power makes inflation an increasingly important 
downside risk to Fiji’s growth outlook heading into 2027.
Source: FBoS, Macrobond, Westpac Economics

WESTPAC PACIFIC ECONOMICS
CREDIT RATING OUTLOOK
S&P negative outlook is concerning
Fiji’s sovereign credit profile has come under renewed pressure
recently, adding another layer of caution to an economy already
navigating softer real growth and elevated external risks.

S&P maintained Fiji’s long-term rating at B+ and short-term
rating at B but revised the outlook from Stable to Negative.
While not a downgrade, it signals increased risk if fiscal
and debt metrics deteriorate. Historically, four of Fiji’s six
negative outlooks since 2006 have resulted in downgrades,
typically during periods of major economic or fiscal stress.
Similar vulnerabilities are resurfacing, with rising government
expenditure and guarantees, alongside Middle East tensions and

inflationary pressures.
The key concern is fiscal. S&P viewed the 2026/27 Budget as
more expansionary than previously expected, effectively slowing
the fiscal consolidation achieved in previous years. It projects
the fiscal deficit to average 4.6% of GDP over 2027–29, including
4.9% in FY2027, with net government debt moving above 70% of
GDP. Once contingent liabilities are incorporated, the broader
public-sector exposure approaches 80% of GDP. This leaves

considerably less fiscal space to absorb another major domestic
or external shock.
In S&P’s latest assessment, contingent liabilities are becoming
increasingly important to the sovereign risk story. Government
exposure through guarantees and other contingent liabilities
adds to the broader fiscal risk, while a widening current account
deficit, elevated imports and rising external debt reinforce the
risks surrounding the outlook. For Fiji, this matters because

the next phase of growth will require substantial investment in
infrastructure, climate resilience and productive capacity.
The negative outlook therefore strengthens the case for a more
credible medium-term fiscal consolidation path by the Fiji
Government. S&P indicated that the outlook could return to
Stable if economic growth and tourism outperform its forecasts,
resulting in narrower fiscal deficits and lower debt ratios, or if
improvements in institutional and policy settings sustainably

strengthen fiscal outcomes and debt levels.
Importantly, the assessment across rating agencies is not
uniformly negative. Moody’s maintained Fiji’s B1 long-term issuer
rating with a Stable outlook, suggesting that despite significant
structural and fiscal challenges, the overall credit profile remains
broadly unchanged and risks remain balanced.
A future downgrade would likely reinforce perceptions of
higher sovereign risk just as Fiji requires greater private and

foreign investment. Maintaining fiscal credibility, containing
contingent liabilities and ensuring that new borrowing supports
productivity-enhancing investment will be increasingly
important. With elevated uncertainty, climate vulnerability and
external shocks remaining additional downside risks, the room
for further fiscal slippage is becoming increasingly limited for the
Fijian Government.
